- **smell-critic**: Adversarial read-only critic. Given an artifact plus smelltest's deterministic findings, it tries to REFUTE the agent's done/verified/cited claims. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User created small business content. user: "Review this from a solopreneur's perspective" assistant: "I'll use the solopreneur agent to evaluate from a one-person business viewpoint—time constraints, budget sensitivity, and self-implementation." &lt;commentary&gt;Solopreneur perspective highlights practical concerns… ([aitytech/agentkits-marketing](https://github.com/aitytech/agentkits-marketing/blob/HEAD/plugins/campaign-manager/agents/solopreneur.md) · ★604 · 4 copies)
- **soloscrum-auditor**: Repo-local audit subagent. Sweeps the soloscrum plugin-distributed spec corpus (skills/, agents/, commands/, CLAUDE.md, README.md) and emits a Markdown report of detected drift across the four rule sets defined in soloscrum-audit-spec-consistency. Read-only — proposes fixes, never edits. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/soloscrum-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-design**: Design agent. Validates feature design, plans subtask decomposition, assigns task types. Use during /soloscrum:validate and /soloscrum:breakdown commands. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/blob/HEAD/agents/soloscrum-design.md) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-dev**: Development agent. Decomposes Issues into Subtasks via the active tracker profile (during /soloscrum:breakdown), and implements code / creates PRs / transitions state for the /soloscrum:develop target (Subtask or no-Subtask Issue per soloscrum-define-branch-commit). Use during /soloscrum:breakdown and /soloscrum:develop commands. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/blob/HEAD/agents/soloscrum-dev.md) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-po**: Product Owner agent. Structures ideas into GitHub Issues, evaluates size, determines priority and SP. Use during /soloscrum:refine command. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/blob/HEAD/agents/soloscrum-po.md) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-review**: Review agent. Reviews PR code quality, verifies DoD with layered AC check (Subtask PR vs Issue-without-Subtasks vs parent Issue intent-level sign-off per soloscrum-define-dod), makes Pass/Fail verdict. Use during /soloscrum:review command. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/blob/HEAD/agents/soloscrum-review.md) · ★1)
- **soloscrum-ui**: UI agent. Creates Figma components, applies design tokens, maintains UI pattern consistency. Use during /soloscrum:design-ui command. ([mew-ton/soloscrum](https://github.com/mew-ton/soloscrum/blob/HEAD/agents/soloscrum-ui.md) · ★1)
- **Solution Architect**: Designs systems, validates architectural patterns, produces ADRs and sequence diagrams. Use for bounded context reviews, technology choices, API contract design, and non-functional requirements. ([doubts-suplab/eeik-bootstrap](https://github.com/doubts-suplab/eeik-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/architect.agent.md) · ★1)
- **Solution Engineer**: Hands-on GIS prototype builder who takes strategy from Technical Consultant and turns it into working demos, proof-of-concepts, and technical validations across the full Esri and open-source stack. ([ericwingens/raysman-claude](https://github.com/ericwingens/raysman-claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gis-solution-engineer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **solution-agent**: Generates principle-based solution sketches from a contradiction card ([SharathSPhD/triz-engine](https://github.com/SharathSPhD/triz-engine/blob/HEAD/triz-engine/agents/solution-agent.md) · ★5)
- **solution-aggregator**: Aggregates parallel solution proposals from the debug pipeline's D2 phase. Reads all `propose.solution-proposer.*.tmp` files, scores each proposal on 5 dimensions (correctness, scope, risk, confidence, testability), and recommends the best fix. Writes ranked analysis to `.agents/tmp/debug/D2-solutions.md`. Use this agent after all solution-proposer agents have written their temp files during the D2 debug phase. &lt;example&gt; Context: 3 solution-proposer agents have completed their proposals for a null pointer bug user: "Aggregate solution proposals and recommend the best fix" assistant: "Spawning… ([kenkenmain/ken-cc-plugins](https://github.com/kenkenmain/ken-cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/ants/agents/solution-aggregator.md) · ★1)
- **solution-aggregator**: Aggregates and ranks parallel solution proposals with trade-off analysis. Use after all solution-proposer agents have written their temp files. ([kenkenmain/ken-cc-plugins](https://github.com/kenkenmain/ken-cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/subagents/agents/solution-aggregator.md) · ★1)
- **solution-alm-engineer**: Use this agent for Power Platform Application Lifecycle Management — pac CLI mastery, source control of unpacked solutions, environment variables, connection references, environment strategy at the solution level (dev/test/prod promotion), and ALM pipelines. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/power-platform/agents/solution-alm-engineer.md) · ★7)
- **solution-analyst**: Runs the mandatory analysis stage. Studies the task and solution path, applies rubric.json as objective floor, proposes approach + exact roles needed + checkpoints. Read-only -- never edits, merges, or spawns. Output goes to approver for sign-off. ([ruslan-shaydullin/crewboss](https://github.com/ruslan-shaydullin/crewboss/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/solution-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **solution-architect**: Use when established requirements and evidence leave multiple viable designs or cross-component boundaries that need explicit trade-offs before implementation. Do not use for a small specified fix or while the root cause is still unknown. ([zebbern/claude-code-guide](https://github.com/zebbern/claude-code-guide/blob/HEAD/agents/.claude/agents/solution-architect.md) · ★4.6k)
- **solution-architect**: Solution Architect — independent design reviewer. Reviews technical designs, migration AgDRs, and feature specs BEFORE the Build phase for architectural soundness (NFRs, patterns, tech debt, decisions, risk, trade-offs, traceability). The non-code analog of the Code Reviewer (Rex). Auto-activates on PRs that touch design artifacts; explicit invocation via /design-review. Canonical role at @roles/architecture/solution-architect.md. ([me2resh/apexyard](https://github.com/me2resh/apexyard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/solution-architect.md) · ★507)
